2026-10-25T10:36:50.5081023Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5081625Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5082269Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5082537Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5083214Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5083659Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5083894Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5084638Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5085522Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5085852Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5086339Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5087167Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5087735Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5088297Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5088530Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5088796Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5089225Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5089483Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5090070Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5090342Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5095561Z             log.debug("Writing the '%s' locale to %s.", lang, fpath)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5096017Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5096205Z             with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5096740Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5097389Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5097572Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5098202Z             msg = "Cannot write language configuration file: {}".format(e.strerror)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5098962Z             raise LanguageInstallationError(msg) from e

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5124068Z def write_x_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, switch_options, x_conf_dir_path, root):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5125012Z     """Write X keyboard layout configuration to the configuration files.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5125501Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5125822Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5126490Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5127424Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5127983Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5128513Z     :param switch_options: list of options for layout switching
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5129117Z     :type switch_options: list(str)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5129751Z     :param x_conf_dir_path: path to directory holding X Layouts configuration
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5130407Z     :type x_conf_dir_path: str
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5130885Z     :param root: root path of the configured system
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5131387Z     :type root: str
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5131724Z     """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5131938Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5132079Z     errors = []
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5132413Z     try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5132803Z         if not os.path.isdir(x_conf_dir_path):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5133343Z             os.makedirs(x_conf_dir_path)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5133785Z     except OSError:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5134328Z         # Non-fatal, systemd-localed may create the directory on its own.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5135230Z         log.debug("Cannot create X Layouts configuration directory %s", x_conf_dir_path)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5135796Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5135958Z     if root != "/":
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5136471Z         # Writing to a different root, we need to save these values, so that
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5137403Z         # we can restore them when we have the file written out.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5138119Z         saved_layouts_variants = localed_wrapper.get_layouts_variants()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5138811Z         saved_options = localed_wrapper.options
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5139164Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5140345Z     # also generates a new conf file.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5140907Z     localed_wrapper.set_layouts(x_layouts, switch_options)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5141308Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5141475Z     if root != "/":
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5141957Z         # Make sure the target directory exists under the given root.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5142867Z         rooted_xconf_dir = os.path.normpath(root + "/" + x_conf_dir_path)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5143495Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5143902Z             if not os.path.isdir(rooted_xconf_dir):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5144440Z                 os.makedirs(rooted_xconf_dir)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5144933Z         except OSError:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5145497Z             errors.append("Cannot create directory {}".format(rooted_xconf_dir))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5146000Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5146164Z         # Copy the file to the chroot.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5146811Z         xconf_file_path = os.path.normpath(x_conf_dir_path + "/" + X_CONF_FILE_NAME)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5148035Z         rooted_xconf_file_path = os.path.normpath(root + "/" + xconf_file_path)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5148707Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5149144Z             shutil.copy2(xconf_file_path, rooted_xconf_file_path)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5149735Z         except OSError as ioerr:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5150380Z             log.error("Cannot copy X layouts configuration file %s to target system: %s.",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5151160Z                       xconf_file_path, ioerr.strerror)

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5151684Z         # Restore the original values.

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5152813Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5152952Z     if errors:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5153406Z         raise KeyboardInstallationError("\n".join(errors))

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5154019Z def write_vc_configuration(vc_keymap, root):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5154641Z     """Write virtual console keyboard mapping configuration.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5155049Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5155302Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5155877Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5156354Z     :param root: root path of the configured system
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5156875Z     :type root: str
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5157451Z     """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5157796Z     try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5158235Z         fpath = os.path.normpath(root + VC_CONF_FILE_PATH)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5158636Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5158916Z         # "eurlatgr" may not be the best choice for all the languages,
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5159617Z         # for example, Russian, Urdu, Serbian, Sindhi and a few more.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5160204Z         # refer: https://bugzilla.redhat.com/show_bug.cgi?id=1919486
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5160754Z         # assuming that LANG is set by now.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5161183Z         vc_fonts = get_locale_console_fonts(os.environ["LANG"])
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5161576Z         vc_font = vc_fonts[0] if vc_fonts else DEFAULT_VC_FONT
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5161803Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5161907Z         with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5162191Z             fobj.write('KEYMAP="%s"\n' % vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5162396Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5162572Z             # systemd now defaults to a font that cannot display non-ascii
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5162993Z             # characters, so we have to tell it to use a better one
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5163334Z             fobj.write('FONT="%s"\n' % vc_font)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5163524Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5163618Z     except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5163946Z         msg = "Cannot write vconsole configuration file: {}".format(e.strerror)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5164358Z         raise KeyboardInstallationError(msg) from e

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5332169Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5333006Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5333573Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5334411Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5335289Z     """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5335578Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5335818Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5336301Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5336654Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5336916Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5337747Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5338340Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5338669Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5339376Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5339871Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5340162Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5341001Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5341697Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5342170Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5343173Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5344175Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5345155Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5346067Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5347353Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5348297Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5348966Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5349681Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5350343Z             #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5350806Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5351613Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5352475Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5353310Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5354239Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5354922Z             #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5355613Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5356290Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5356665Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5357796Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5358636Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5359398Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5359932Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5360238Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5360880Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5361470Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5362075Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5362802Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5363481Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5364084Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5364773Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5367483Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5368518Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5369452Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5370223Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5370727Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5370945Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5371312Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5371459Z     @property

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5372952Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5404074Z         self._set_layouts(layouts_variants, options, convert)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5404765Z         log.debug("Storing layouts for compositor configured by user")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5405429Z         self._user_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5405801Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5406130Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5406611Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5406806Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5406974Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5408995Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5409614Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5410467Z         if options is None:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5410912Z             options = self.options
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5411355Z             log.debug(
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5412031Z                 "Keyboard layouts for compositor are missing options. Use compositor options: %s",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5412797Z                 options,
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5413177Z             )
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5413381Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5413809Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5415042Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5415779Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5416131Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5416802Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5417942Z             options,
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5418303Z         )
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5418986Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5419476Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5419652Z         # store configuration from user
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5420242Z         super().set_layouts(layouts_variants, options, convert)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5420670Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5420866Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5421444Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5421846Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5422209Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5423042Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5423511Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5424158Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5424865Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5425325Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5425883Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5426396Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5426742Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5428345Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5429005Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5429334Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5429473Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5429911Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5430491Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5430962Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5431337Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5431909Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5432569Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5433042Z             return False
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5433282Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5433420Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5433839Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5434557Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5435086Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5435455Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5436151Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5436420Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5436626Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5437452Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5437875Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5438302Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5438539Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5438799Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5439213Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5439545Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5440178Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5440575Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5441351Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5442059Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5442287Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5442458Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5442971Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5443369Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5443771Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5444749Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5445973Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5446804Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5447450Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5447777Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5448592Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5449132Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5449708Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5450082Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5450459Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5450992Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5451218Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5451391Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5452152Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5453936Z             return False
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5454182Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5454305Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5454619Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5454950Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5455216Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5455516Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5455755Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5455841Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5456131Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5456843Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5463660Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5464166Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5464570Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5464970Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5465395Z             return False

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5626052Z from pyanaconda.anaconda_loggers import get_module_logger
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5626301Z from pyanaconda.core.constants import DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5626689Z from pyanaconda.modules.localization.live_keyboard import get_live_keyboard_instance
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5626696Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5626831Z log = get_module_logger(__name__)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5626837Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5626841Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627388Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627573Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627583Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627720Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627925Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628069Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628356Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628502Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628692Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628993Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5629312Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5629503Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5629509Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5629827Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630006Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630230Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630389Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630639Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630963Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5631192Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5631348Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5631478Z     """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5631639Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632062Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632374Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632383Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632592Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632992Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633181Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633509Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633740Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633747Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633933Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5634370Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5634549Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5634556Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5634736Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635205Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635212Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635375Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635381Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635386Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635737Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635882Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5636134Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5636567Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5636738Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5646533Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5648383Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5648520Z #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5648666Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5649028Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5649380Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5649652Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5650138Z #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5650278Z set -eu
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5650285Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5650580Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5650877Z do
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651036Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651167Z         in
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651324Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651483Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651735Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651884Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652025Z     esac
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652160Z done
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652168Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652443Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652451Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652617Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652775Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652902Z then

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5653874Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654041Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654324Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654534Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654666Z     #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655000Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655180Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655421Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655621Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655760Z     else
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5656105Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5656558Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5656565Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657340Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657540Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657707Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657837Z       fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657972Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657979Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5658176Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5658645Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5658658Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5658941Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659119Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659442Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659622Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659800Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659947Z     else
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660188Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660339Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660346Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660581Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660734Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660865Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660871Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5661020Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5661521Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5661855Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5661991Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662131Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662137Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662339Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662523Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662823Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662955Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663091Z         fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663229Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663393Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663526Z     done
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663532Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663968Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5664391Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5664565Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5664871Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665268Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665420Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665553Z     else
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665853Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665997Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666131Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666264Z }

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666427Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666572Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666578Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666943Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5667593Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5667931Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5667940Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5668193Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5668372Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5668517Z     then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5668850Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5669018Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5669153Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5669452Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5669655Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5669945Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5670208Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5670405Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5670411Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5670920Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5671092Z     # set environment for Firefox
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5671561Z     BROWSER_ENV=(MOZ_APP_TITLE="" MOZ_APP_REMOTINGNAME="liveinst" MOZ_GTK_TITLEBAR_DECORATION=client)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5671567Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672098Z     BROWSER=(/usr/bin/env "${BROWSER_ENV[@]}" /usr/bin/firefox --new-instance --profile "${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672105Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672258Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672386Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672392Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672397Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672563Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672689Z then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672844Z     /bin/sleep infinity &
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5672993Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5673119Z else

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5673720Z     HOME_DIR=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f2)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5673733Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5674124Z     if type nc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5674273Z         for _ in `seq 10`; do
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5674448Z             nc -z "$WEBUI_ADDRESS" 80 && break
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5674593Z             sleep 0.5;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5674727Z         done
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5674858Z     else

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5675151Z         sleep 3
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5675293Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5675299Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5676027Z     readarray -t user_environment < <(pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" env XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}" systemctl --user show-environment)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5676035Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5677665Z     else

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5678213Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5679165Z     HOME="$BROWSER_HOME" XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP=GNOME \

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684131Z action() {

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684280Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684409Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684580Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685727Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685870Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686010Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686016Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686281Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686449Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686734Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686880Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5687364Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5687511Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5687710Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5687890Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688200Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688385Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688651Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688811Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688974Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689078Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689217Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689314Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689320Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689420Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689588Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689726Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689731Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689912Z case "$test_tty" in

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691526Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692423Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692549Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692665Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692670Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692791Z echo_success() {

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693070Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693259Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693565Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693744Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693888Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694026Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694164Z     return 0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694297Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694303Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694442Z echo_failure() {

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694754Z     printf '%s' "["

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695447Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695585Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695721Z     return 1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695850Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695862Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696000Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696153Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696304Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696453Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696619Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696773Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696913Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5697393Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5697590Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5697724Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5697873Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698030Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698200Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698340Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698466Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698676Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698896Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699023Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699155Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699317Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699511Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699651Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699800Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699970Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700136Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700274Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700406Z         *)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700689Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700855Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700985Z     esac
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5701126Z }

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716065Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716161Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716269Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716379Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716493Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716566Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716569Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716724Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716839Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5717308Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5720287Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5720487Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5720754Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721123Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721259Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721454Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721598Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721853Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721861Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722042Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722216Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722352Z     echo
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722537Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722926Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723087Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723221Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723510Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723763Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723843Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723847Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723942Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724073Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724252Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724434Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724568Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724688Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724795Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724883Z }

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724990Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725088Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725171Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725329Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725460Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725534Z fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725538Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5076545Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5081020Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5081623Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5082079Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5082534Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5083210Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5083655Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5083890Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5084632Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5085517Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5085841Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5086336Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5087162Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5087729Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5088293Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5088527Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5088793Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5089222Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5089468Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5090064Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5090338Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5095557Z             log.debug("Writing the '%s' locale to %s.", lang, fpath)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5096011Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5096201Z             with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5096735Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5097381Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5097567Z         except OSError as e:

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5332164Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5333001Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5333569Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5334397Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5335281Z     """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5335574Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5336298Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5336651Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5338151Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5338664Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5339373Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5339868Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5340158Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5340994Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5341690Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5342162Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5343167Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5344171Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5345152Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5346063Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5347333Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5348290Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5348962Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5349677Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5350340Z             #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5350802Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5351609Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5352471Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5353307Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5354235Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5354919Z             #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5355602Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5356286Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5356661Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5357790Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5358633Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5359395Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5359929Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5360234Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5360877Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5361466Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5362072Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5362798Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5363468Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5364080Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5364769Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5365551Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5366411Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5367477Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5368507Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5369447Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5370207Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5370722Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5370941Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5371307Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5406127Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5406608Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5406805Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5406971Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5408987Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5409597Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5410463Z         if options is None:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5410907Z             options = self.options
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5411350Z             log.debug(

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5412792Z                 options,
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5413172Z             )
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5413377Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5413804Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5415037Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5415775Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5416127Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5416797Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5417930Z             options,
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5418299Z         )
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5418788Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5419472Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5419648Z         # store configuration from user

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5420666Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5420862Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5421440Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5421843Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5422204Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5423038Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5423507Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5424140Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5424859Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5425319Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5425860Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5426392Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5426738Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5428334Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5429002Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5429330Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5429469Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5429908Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5430488Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5430958Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5431334Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5431905Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5432565Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5433039Z             return False
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5433279Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5433417Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5433829Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5434545Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5435083Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5435452Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5436147Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5436416Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5436622Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5437444Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5437871Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5438299Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5438536Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5438796Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5439210Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5439542Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5440175Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5440571Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5441332Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5442055Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5442284Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5442454Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5442968Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5443366Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5443767Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5444745Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5445968Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5446801Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5447439Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5447752Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5448421Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5449128Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5449704Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5450077Z         """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5450457Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5450988Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5451215Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5451388Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5452148Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5453927Z             return False
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5454179Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5454303Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5454617Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5454948Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5455213Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5455513Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5455743Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5455838Z         try:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5456127Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5456836Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5463648Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5464161Z             return True
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5464566Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5464967Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5465393Z             return False

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5626839Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627383Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627570Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627576Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627718Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5627922Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628065Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628353Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628500Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628690Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5628989Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5629300Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5629500Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5629507Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5629824Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630002Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630227Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630386Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630636Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5630960Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5631189Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5631346Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5631475Z     """
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5631636Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632053Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632224Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632380Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632589Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5632988Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633177Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633504Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633736Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633745Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5633930Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5634366Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5634545Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5634553Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5634733Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635193Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635210Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635371Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635378Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635383Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635734Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5635879Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5636131Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5636562Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5636735Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5636890Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5637438Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5637827Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5637988Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5637995Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5638161Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5638169Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5646529Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5646889Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5648005Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5648377Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5648515Z #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5648661Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5649024Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5649363Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5649648Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5650135Z #

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5650282Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5650873Z do
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651032Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651163Z         in
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651321Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651479Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651731Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5651881Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652021Z     esac
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652157Z done
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652165Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5652449Z

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5653871Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654038Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654320Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654530Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654663Z     #
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5654996Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655169Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655417Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655618Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5655756Z     else
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5656102Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5656555Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5656563Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657333Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657536Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657704Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657835Z       fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657968Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5657977Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5658173Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5658463Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5658654Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5658936Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659115Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659436Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659617Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659796Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5659943Z     else
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660185Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660336Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660343Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660571Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660731Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660862Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5660869Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5661017Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5661517Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5661844Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5661988Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662128Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662134Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662336Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662520Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662820Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5662952Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663088Z         fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663226Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663390Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663518Z     done
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663530Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5663965Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5664387Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5664555Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5664867Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665265Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665417Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665550Z     else
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665850Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5665994Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666128Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666261Z }

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666424Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666569Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666576Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5666939Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5667586Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5667916Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5681767Z #!/bin/sh
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5681961Z # Functions to be used with livesys-* scripts
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5681968Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5682167Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5682344Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5682636Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5682804Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5682934Z fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5683137Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5683413Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5683550Z strstr() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5683693Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5683822Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5683828Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5683989Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684129Z action() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684265Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684271Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684407Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684577Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684718Z     shift
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5684860Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685020Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685152Z     else
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685319Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685448Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685584Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685724Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685867Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5685999Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686014Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686277Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686447Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686731Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5686876Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5687357Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5687507Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5687707Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5687887Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688196Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688375Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688648Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688808Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5688972Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689071Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689210Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689312Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689317Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689419Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689586Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689724Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689729Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689825Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5689910Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690004Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690087Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690171Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690262Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690345Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690432Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690513Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690659Z         ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690796Z esac
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690813Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5690995Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691125Z success() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691286Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691407Z     return 0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691515Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691524Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691664Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691781Z failure() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5691892Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692043Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692420Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692546Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692663Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692668Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692788Z echo_success() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5692939Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693067Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693256Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693425Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693742Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5693885Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694023Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694162Z     return 0
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694294Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694301Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694440Z echo_failure() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694610Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694751Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5694936Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_FAILURE
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695108Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695293Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695444Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695582Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695719Z     return 1
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695847Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695853Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5695997Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696149Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696294Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696450Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696617Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696770Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5696910Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5697388Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5697586Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5697720Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5697870Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698026Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698197Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698337Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698464Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698673Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5698888Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699020Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699152Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699314Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699500Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699647Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699797Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5699967Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700133Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700271Z             ;;
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700403Z         *)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700686Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700852Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5700982Z     esac
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5701123Z }

2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716063Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716159Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716268Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716378Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716491Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716564Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716568Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716722Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5716828Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5717302Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5720275Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5720484Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5720751Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721119Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721256Z     fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721450Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721595Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721850Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5721858Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722038Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722213Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722350Z     echo
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722534Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5722915Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723084Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723218Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723507Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723760Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723841Z   fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723846Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5723941Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724071Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724250Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724432Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724566Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724686Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724794Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724876Z }
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724886Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5724989Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725087Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725170Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725327Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725457Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725532Z fi
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725537Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5725720Z # if we have a persistent /home, then we want to go ahead and mount it
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5726045Z { ! strstr "$cmdline" nopersistenthome && [ "$homedev" ]; } &&
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5726202Z   action "Mounting persistent /home" mountPersistentHome
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5726207Z
2026-10-25T10:36:50.5726301Z [ "$configdone" ] && exit 0
